They validate heavily through the live surface instead of accepting code-only claims: Chrome for local web previews and ChatGPT Images, Android Emulator `FlashPlay_API35:5554` plus ADB for app QA, Finder for local asset provenance, and Telegram for APK/image handoff. Good collaboration here means: use the visible app/browser/emulator state as the truth source, keep changes incremental, preserve the working flow while fixing the specific problem, and carry tasks through rebuild/reopen/verification instead of stopping at “edited files.” The user often has adjacent tabs open, but the durable pattern is pragmatic iteration with concrete checks and minimal hand-waving. [105] assistant: 当前这个 Codex 环境里：

- **没有暴露 Blender 专用 MCP 接口**
- 本机也 **没有安装 `blender` CLI**
- `/Applications` 里没有 `Blender.app`

所以现在不能直接调用 Blender 建模。

可行方案有三种：

1. **安装 Blender 后用 CLI**
   安装后可用：

   ```bash
   /Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender --background --python model.py
   ```

   我可以生成 `model.py`，用 Blender Python 自动建模、材质、灯光、相机，并导出 `.blend` / `.glb` / `.obj`。

2. **用 Three.js / glTF 先生成模型**
   如果目标是网页或游戏，我可以直接生成 `.glb`、Three.js 场景或 procedural mesh，不依赖 Blender。

3. **接入 Blender MCP**
   如果你安装一个 Blender MCP server，并让 Codex 可见，我就可以走 MCP 操作 Blender。但当前会话没有这个工具。

结论：**Codex 可以写 Blender Python 并通过 CLI 自动建模，但你这台环境目前缺 Blender。** 安装 Blender 后就能直接跑。

[105] assistant: 当前这个 Codex 环境里：

- **没有暴露 Blender 专用 MCP 接口**
- 本机也 **没有安装 `blender` CLI**
- `/Applications` 里没有 `Blender.app`

所以现在不能直接调用 Blender 建模。

可行方案有三种：

1. **安装 Blender 后用 CLI**
   安装后可用：

   ```bash
   /Applications/Blender.app/Contents/MacOS/Blender --background --python model.py
   ```

   我可以生成 `model.py`，用 Blender Python 自动建模、材质、灯光、相机，并导出 `.blend` / `.glb` / `.obj`。

2. **用 Three.js / glTF 先生成模型**
   如果目标是网页或游戏，我可以直接生成 `.glb`、Three.js 场景或 procedural mesh，不依赖 Blender。

3. **接入 Blender MCP**
   如果你安装一个 Blender MCP server，并让 Codex 可见，我就可以走 MCP 操作 Blender。但当前会话没有这个工具。

结论：**Codex 可以写 Blender Python 并通过 CLI 自动建模，但你这台环境目前缺 Blender。** 安装 Blender 后就能直接跑。

+import math

+
+import bpy
+

+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()

+    material = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    material.use_nodes = True
+    bsdf = material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.45
+    bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.05
+    return material
+
+
+def main():
+    clear_scene()
+
+    material = create_material("Codex Test Blue", (0.08, 0.34, 0.95, 1.0))
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=2.0, location=(0, 0, 1))
+    cube = bpy.context.object
+    cube.name = "Codex_Blender_CLI_Test_Cube"
+    cube.data.materials.append(material)
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_plane_add(size=6.0, location=(0, 0, 0))
+    plane = bpy.context.object
+    plane.name = "Ground"
+    plane.data.materials.append(create_material("Matte Ground", (0.18, 0.18, 0.18, 1.0)))
+
+    bpy.ops.object.light_add(type="AREA", location=(2.5, -3.0, 5.0))
+    light = bpy.context.object
+    light.name = "Key_Area_Light"
+    light.data.energy = 450
+    light.data.size = 4
+
+    bpy.ops.object.camera_add(location=(4.0, -5.0, 3.2), rotation=(math.radians(60), 0, math.radians(38)))
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = bpy.context.object
+
+    bpy.context.scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    bpy.ops.wm.save_as_mainfile(filepath=str(OUT_BLEND))
+    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(filepath=str(OUT_GLB), export_format="GLB")
+
+    print(f"saved: {OUT_BLEND}")
+    print(f"exported: {OUT_GLB}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
*** End Patch

+import math
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+OUT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+OUT_BLEND = OUT_DIR / "test_cube.blend"
+OUT_GLB = OUT_DIR / "test_cube.glb"
+
+
+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def create_material(name, color):
+    material = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    material.use_nodes = True
+    bsdf = material.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.45
+    bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = 0.05
+    return material
+
+
+def main():
+    clear_scene()
+
+    material = create_material("Codex Test Blue", (0.08, 0.34, 0.95, 1.0))
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=2.0, location=(0, 0, 1))
+    cube = bpy.context.object
+    cube.name = "Codex_Blender_CLI_Test_Cube"
+    cube.data.materials.append(material)
+
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_plane_add(size=6.0, location=(0, 0, 0))
+    plane = bpy.context.object
+    plane.name = "Ground"
+    plane.data.materials.append(create_material("Matte Ground", (0.18, 0.18, 0.18, 1.0)))
+
+    bpy.ops.object.light_add(type="AREA", location=(2.5, -3.0, 5.0))
+    light = bpy.context.object
+    light.name = "Key_Area_Light"
+    light.data.energy = 450
+    light.data.size = 4
+
+    bpy.ops.object.camera_add(location=(4.0, -5.0, 3.2), rotation=(math.radians(60), 0, math.radians(38)))
+    bpy.context.scene.camera = bpy.context.object
+
+    bpy.context.scene.render.engine = "BLENDER_EEVEE_NEXT"
+    bpy.ops.wm.save_as_mainfile(filepath=str(OUT_BLEND))
+    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(filepath=str(OUT_GLB), export_format="GLB")
+
+    print(f"saved: {OUT_BLEND}")
+    print(f"exported: {OUT_GLB}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
*** End Patch

# Browser

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

Before using this skill for the first time in the current conversation context, read the entire `SKILL.md` file in one read. Do not use a partial range such as `sed -n '1,220p'`; read through the end of the file. Do not mention this internal skill-loading step to the user.

## Bootstrap

If setup or recovery is needed, describe it naturally as connecting to the browser or retrying the browser connection.

The `browser-client` module is the core entry point for browser use, and is available under `scripts/browser-client.mjs` in this plugin's root directory. ALWAYS import it using an absolute path.
IMPORTANT: If this path cannot be found, stop and rep<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="2522" />ht.

Summarize it naturally for the user, for example: "Browser use was stopped in the extension." Avoid internal terms like turn_id, runtime, retry, or plugin error text unless the user asks for details.
* When testing a user's local app on `localhost`, `127.0.0.1`, `::1`, or another local development URL in a framework that does not support hot reloading or hot reloading is disabled, call `tab.reload()` after code or build changes before verifying the UI. After reloading, take a fresh DOM snapshot or screenshot before continuing.
* Do not brute-force undocumented site search URLs, query parameter variants, search engine query grids, or candidate URL arrays unless the user explicitly asks for exhaustive coverage.
* If a guessed URL, search query, or candidate page fails, try at most one new approach. After that, switch to visible page navigation, the site's own search UI, or give the best current answer with uncertainty.

+import math
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+OUT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+OUT_BLEND = OUT_DIR / "car_model.blend"
+OUT_GLB = OUT_DIR / "car_model.glb"
+BODY_TEXTURE = OUT_DIR / "car_body_texture.png"
+
+
+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_texture():
+    width, height = 1024, 512
+    image = bpy.data.images.new("Car Body Livery Texture", width=width, height=height, alpha=True)
+    pixels = [0.0] * (width * height * 4)
+
+    for y in range(height):
+        for x in range(width):
+            u = x / (width - 1)
+            v = y / (height - 1)
+
+            # Deep blue base with subtle panel shading.
+            shade = 0.88 + 0.08 * math.sin(u * math.pi * 8) * math.sin(v * math.pi * 3)
+            r, g, b = 0.035 * shade, 0.19 * shade, 0.55 * shade
+
+            # White racing stripe and cyan pinstripes.
+            if 0.465 < u < 0.535:
+                r, g, b = 0.92, 0.94, 0.90
+            if 0.445 < u < 0.455 or 0.545 < u < 0.555:
+                r, g, b = 0.02, 0.85, 0.95
+
+            # Door / hood panel lines.
+            if abs(u - 0.25) < 0.004 or abs(u - 0.78) < 0.004 or abs(v - 0.18) < 0.004:
+                r, g, b = 0.01, 0.025, 0.055
+
+            i = (y * width + x) * 4
+            pixels[i : i + 4] = (r, g, b, 1.0)
+
+    image.pixels = pixels
+    image.filepath_raw = str(BODY_TEXTURE)
+    image.file_format = "PNG"
+    image.save()
+    return image
+
+
+def material_principled(name, color, roughness=0.45, metallic=0.0):
+    mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    mat.use_nodes = True
+    bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
+    bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
+    return mat
+
+

Use this skill for browser automation tasks such as inspecting pages, navigating, testing local apps, clicking, typing, taking screenshots, and reading visible page state. After setup, select the `iab` browser.

If this plugin is listed as available in the session, treat that as mandatory reading before browser work. Open and follow this skill before saying that Browser is unavailable and before falling back to standalone Playwright or Computer Use.

Do not skip this skill just because Computer Use MCP tool calls are directly visible or appear easier to invoke. The presence of Computer Use tools is not evidence that Computer Use is the preferred browser surface.

+import math
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+OUT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+OUT_BLEND = OUT_DIR / "car_model.blend"
+OUT_GLB = OUT_DIR / "car_model.glb"
+BODY_TEXTURE = OUT_DIR / "car_body_texture.png"
+
+
+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_texture():
+    width, height = 1024, 512
+    image = bpy.data.images.new("Car Body Livery Texture", width=width, height=height, alpha=True)
+    pixels = [0.0] * (width * height * 4)
+
+    for y in range(height):
+        for x in range(width):
+            u = x / (width - 1)
+            v = y / (height - 1)
+
+            # Deep blue base with subtle panel shading.
+            shade = 0.88 + 0.08 * math.sin(u * math.pi * 8) * math.sin(v * math.pi * 3)
+            r, g, b = 0.035 * shade, 0.19 * shade, 0.55 * shade
+
+            # White racing stripe and cyan pinstripes.
+            if 0.465 < u < 0.535:
+                r, g, b = 0.92, 0.94, 0.90
+            if 0.445 < u < 0.455 or 0.545 < u < 0.555:
+                r, g, b = 0.02, 0.85, 0.95
+
+            # Door / hood panel lines.

+                r, g, b = 0.01, 0.025, 0.055
+
+            i = (y * width + x) * 4
+            pixels[i : i + 4] = (r, g, b, 1.0)
+
+    image.pixels = pixels
+    image.filepath_raw = str(BODY_TEXTURE)
+    image.file_format = "PNG"
+    image.save()
+    return image
+
+
+def material_principled(name, color, roughness=0.45, metallic=0.0):
+    mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    mat.use_nodes = True
+    bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = color
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
+    bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
+    return mat
+

---
name: three-webgl-game
description: Implement browser-game runtimes with plain Three.js. Use when the user wants imperative scene control in TypeScript or Vite with GLB assets, loaders, physics, and low-level WebGL debugging.
---

# Three WebGL Game

## Overview

Use this skill for the default non-React 3D path in the plugin. This is not generic WebGL advice. It is an opinionated stack for browser 3D work:

- `three`
- TypeScript
- Vite
- GLB or glTF 2.0 assets
- Three.js loaders such as `GLTFLoader`, `DRACOLoader`, and `KTX2Loader`
- Rapier JS for physics
- SpectorJS for GPU and frame debugging
- DOM overlays for HUD, menus, and settings

Use this skill when the project wants direct scene, camera, renderer, and game-loop control. If the app already lives in React, route to `../react-three-fiber-game/SKILL.md` instead.

## Use This Skill When

- the app is plain TypeScript or Vite rather than React-first
- the project wants direct imperative control over the render loop
- the user asks for Three.js specifically
- the runtime needs engine-like control over scene, camera, loaders, and physics

## Do Not Use This Skill When

- the 3D scene lives inside an existing React app
- the main problem is shipped-asset optimization rather than runtime code
- the user explicitly chose Babylon.js or PlayCanvas

## Core Rules

1. Keep simulation state outside Three.js objects.
   - Game rules, AI, quest state, timers, and progression should not live inside meshes or materials.
2. Treat the render graph as an adapter.
   - Scene graph, cameras, materials, loaders, and post-processing are view concerns layered over simulation state.
3. Keep camera behavior explicit.
   - Orbit, follow, chase, rail, and first-person styles each need their own control boundary.
4. Keep UI out of WebGL unless the presentation absolutely depends on it.
   - Menus, HUD, invento<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="485" />ible optimization as part of the asset pipeline, not as a random runtime patch.
- Use `KTX2Loader` for compressed textures when the asset pipeline provides them.
- Prefer built-in Three.js render and post-processing utilities first. Add heavier abstraction only when the project actually needs it.
- Keep post-processing optional and measurable. Bloom and color effects should not hide gameplay readability.

## Shader and Material Guidance

- Start with standard Three.js materials and correct lighting before reaching for custom shaders.
- Use custom shaders only when the visual target genuinely needs them.
- Keep shader parameters driven by game state, not by incidental scene mutations.
- If a material stack gets complex, isolate it behind material factories instead of scattering shader setup across scene code.

## Browser Safety

- Handle resize explicitly.
- Expect WebGL context loss and recovery.
- Keep a fallback or degraded mode in mind for fragile GPU paths.
- Watch texture size, geometry count, draw-call growth, and post-processing cost.
- Use SpectorJS when the scene behaves incorrectly or frame cost is unclear.

## Scope Warning

Do not claim that this plugin offers equal 3D depth to the Phaser track. It supports serious 3D implementation, but the plugin is still 2D-first overall.

## References

- Shared architecture: `../web-game-foundations/SKILL.md`
- Frontend direction: `../game-ui-frontend/SKILL.md`
- 3D HUD layout patterns: `../../references/three-hud-layout-patterns.md`
- Three.js ecosystem: `../../references/threejs-stack.md`
- Three.js structure: `../../references/three-webgl-architecture.md`
- Vanilla starter: `../../references/threejs-vanilla-starter.md`
- GLB loader starter: `../../references/gltf-loading-starter.md`
- Rapier starter: `../../references/rapier-integration-starter.md`
- 3D asset pipeline: `../../references/web-3d-asset-pipeline.md`
- WebGL debugging and perf: `../../references/webgl-debugging-and-performance.md`

  <body>
    <div id="game"></div>
    <div class="hud">
      <div class="chip">Arrow keys: drive / steer</div>
      <div id="speed" class="chip speed">0 km/h</div>
    </div>

    <script type="module">
      import * as THREE from "three";
      import { GLTFLoader } from "three/addons/loaders/GLTFLoader.js";

      const root = document.querySelector("#game");
      const speedLabel = document.querySelector("#speed");

      const scene = new THREE.Scene();
      scene<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="240" />  } else if (keys.has("ArrowDown")) {
          car.speed -= car.brake * dt;
        } else {
          const sign = Math.sign(car.speed);
          car.speed -= sign * car.drag * dt;
          if (Math.sign(car.speed) !== sign) car.speed = 0;
        }

        car.speed = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(car.speed, car.maxReverse, car.maxForward);

        const steerInput = (keys.has("ArrowLeft") ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has("ArrowRight") ? 1 : 0);
        const steerPower = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(car.speed) / 6, 0.25, 1);
        car.heading += steerInput * car.steer * steerPower * dt * Math.sign(car.speed || 1);

        const forward = new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(car.heading), 0, -Math.sin(car.heading));
        const nextPosition = carRoot.position.clone().addScaledVector(forward, car.speed * dt);
        clampToPlatform(nextPosition);

        if (hitsObstacle(nextPosition)) {
          car.speed *= -0.25;
        } else {
          carRoot.position.copy(nextPosition);
        }

        carRoot.rotation.y = -car.heading;

        const cameraBack = new THREE.Vector3(-Math.cos(car.heading), 0, Math.sin(car.heading));
        targetCamera.copy(carRoot.position).addScaledVector(cameraBack, 8).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 5.2, 0));
        camera.position.lerp(targetCamera, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
        lookTarget.copy(carRoot.position).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.0, 0));
        camera.lookAt(lookTarget);

        speedLabel.textContent = `${Math.round(Math.abs(car.speed) * 8)} km/h`;
      }

      function animate() {
        const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.033);
        update(dt);
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }

      window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
        camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
      });

      animate();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

---
name: three-webgl-game
description: Implement browser-game runtimes with plain Three.js. Use when the user wants imperative scene control in TypeScript or Vite with GLB assets, loaders, physics, and low-level WebGL debugging.
---

# Three WebGL Game

## Overview

Use this skill for the default non-React 3D path in the plugin. This is not generic WebGL advice. It is an opinionated stack for browser 3D work:

- `three`
- TypeScript
- Vite
- GLB or glTF 2.0 assets
- Three.js loaders such as `GLTFLoader`, `DRACOLoader`, and `KTX2Loader`
- Rapier JS for physics
- SpectorJS for GPU and frame debugging
- DOM overlays for HUD, menus, and settings

Use this skill when the project wants direct scene, camera, renderer, and game-loop control. If the app already lives in React, route to `../react-three-fiber-game/SKILL.md` instead.

## Use This Skill When

- the app is plain TypeScript or Vite rather than React-first
- the project wants direct imperative control over the render loop
- the user asks for Three.js specifically
- the runtime needs engine-like control over scene, camera, loaders, and physics

## Do Not Use This Skill When

- the 3D scene lives inside an existing React app
- the main problem is shipped-asset optimization rather than runtime code
- the user explicitly chose Babylon.js or PlayCanvas

## Core Rules

1. Keep simulation state outside Three.js objects.
   - Game rules, AI, quest state, timers, and progression should not live inside meshes or materials.
2. Treat the render graph as an adapter.
   - Scene graph, cameras, materials, loaders, and post-processing are view concerns layered over simulation state.
3. Keep camera behavior explicit.
   - Orbit, follow, chase, rail, and first-person styles each need their own control boundary.
4. Keep UI out of WebGL unless the presentation absolutely depends on it.
   - Menus, HUD, invento<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="485" />ible optimization as part of the asset pipeline, not as a random runtime patch.
- Use `KTX2Loader` for compressed textures when the asset pipeline provides them.
- Prefer built-in Three.js render and post-processing utilities first. Add heavier abstraction only when the project actually needs it.
- Keep post-processing optional and measurable. Bloom and color effects should not hide gameplay readability.

## Shader and Material Guidance

- Start with standard Three.js materials and correct lighting before reaching for custom shaders.
- Use custom shaders only when the visual target genuinely needs them.
- Keep shader parameters driven by game state, not by incidental scene mutations.
- If a material stack gets complex, isolate it behind material factories instead of scattering shader setup across scene code.

## Browser Safety

- Handle resize explicitly.
- Expect WebGL context loss and recovery.
- Keep a fallback or degraded mode in mind for fragile GPU paths.
- Watch texture size, geometry count, draw-call growth, and post-processing cost.
- Use SpectorJS when the scene behaves incorrectly or frame cost is unclear.

## Scope Warning

Do not claim that this plugin offers equal 3D depth to the Phaser track. It supports serious 3D implementation, but the plugin is still 2D-first overall.

## References

- Shared architecture: `../web-game-foundations/SKILL.md`
- Frontend direction: `../game-ui-frontend/SKILL.md`
- 3D HUD layout patterns: `../../references/three-hud-layout-patterns.md`
- Three.js ecosystem: `../../references/threejs-stack.md`
- Three.js structure: `../../references/three-webgl-architecture.md`
- Vanilla starter: `../../references/threejs-vanilla-starter.md`
- GLB loader starter: `../../references/gltf-loading-starter.md`
- Rapier starter: `../../references/rapier-integration-starter.md`
- 3D asset pipeline: `../../references/web-3d-asset-pipeline.md`
- WebGL debugging and perf: `../../references/webgl-debugging-and-performance.md`

    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <title>Car Platform Drive</title>
    <style>
      html,
      body {
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        margin: 0;
        overflow: hidden;
        background: #0d1016;
        color: #f1eee5;
        font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Consolas, monospace;
      }

      #game {
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
      }

      .hud {
        position: fixed;
        top: 14px;
        left: 14px;
        z-index: 2;
        display: flex;
        gap: 8px;
        align-items: center;
        pointer-events: none;
      }

      .chip {
        border: 1px solid rgba(117, 224, 255, 0.38);
        background: rgba(6, 10, 18, 0.76);
        color: #dcecf5;
        padding: 7px 10px;
        border-radius: 4px;
        box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.24);
        backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
        font-size: 13px;
      }

      .speed {
        min-width: 96px;
        color: #83f7c9;
      }
    </style>
    <script type="importmap">
      {
        "imports": {
          "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.165.0/build/three.module.js",
          "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.165.0/examples/jsm/"
        }
      }
    </script>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div id="game"></div>
    <div class="hud">
      <div class="chip">Arrow keys: drive / steer</div>
      <div id="speed" class="chip speed">0 km/h</div>
    </div>

    <script type="module">
      import * as THREE from "three";
      import { GLTFLoader } from "three/addons/loaders/GLTFLoader.js";

      const root = document.querySelector("#game");
      const speedLabel = document.querySelector("#speed");

      const scene = new THREE.Scene();
      scene<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="240" />  } else if (keys.has("ArrowDown")) {
          car.speed -= car.brake * dt;
        } else {
          const sign = Math.sign(car.speed);
          car.speed -= sign * car.drag * dt;
          if (Math.sign(car.speed) !== sign) car.speed = 0;
        }

        car.speed = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(car.speed, car.maxReverse, car.maxForward);

        const steerInput = (keys.has("ArrowLeft") ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has("ArrowRight") ? 1 : 0);
        const steerPower = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(car.speed) / 6, 0.25, 1);
        car.heading += steerInput * car.steer * steerPower * dt * Math.sign(car.speed || 1);

        const forward = new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(car.heading), 0, -Math.sin(car.heading));
        const nextPosition = carRoot.position.clone().addScaledVector(forward, car.speed * dt);
        clampToPlatform(nextPosition);

        if (hitsObstacle(nextPosition)) {
          car.speed *= -0.25;
        } else {
          carRoot.position.copy(nextPosition);
        }

        carRoot.rotation.y = -car.heading;

        const cameraBack = new THREE.Vector3(-Math.cos(car.heading), 0, Math.sin(car.heading));
        targetCamera.copy(carRoot.position).addScaledVector(cameraBack, 8).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 5.2, 0));
        camera.position.lerp(targetCamera, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
        lookTarget.copy(carRoot.position).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.0, 0));
        camera.lookAt(lookTarget);

        speedLabel.textContent = `${Math.round(Math.abs(car.speed) * 8)} km/h`;
      }

      function animate() {
        const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.033);
        update(dt);
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }

      window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
        camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
      });

      animate();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+SRC = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/animated-characters-3"
+FBX = SRC / "Model/characterMedium.fbx"
+SKIN = SRC / "Skins/humanFemaleA.png"
+OUT = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/female_character.glb"
+
+
+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_skin_material():
+    mat = bpy.data.materials.new("Kenney Human Female Skin")
+    mat.use_nodes = True
+    nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
+    links = mat.node_tree.links
+    bsdf = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexImage")
+    tex.image = bpy.data.images.load(str(SKIN))
+    tex.extension = "CLIP"
+    links.new(tex.outputs["Color"], bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.72
+    return mat
+
+
+def main():
+    clear_scene()
+    bpy.ops.import_scene.fbx(filepath=str(FBX))
+    mat = make_skin_material()
+
+    for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
+        if obj.type == "MESH":
+            obj.name = f"Female_{obj.name}"
+            obj.data.materials.clear()
+            obj.data.materials.append(mat)
+            obj.location.x = 0
+            obj.location.y = 0
+            obj.location.z = 0
+            obj.modifiers.new("Female_WeightedNormals", "WEIGHTED_NORMAL")
+
+    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
+        filepath=str(OUT),
+        export_format="GLB",
+        export_materials="EXPORT",
+        export_yup=True,
+    )
+    print(f"exported: {OUT}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+SRC = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/animated-characters-3"
+FBX = SRC / "Model/characterMedium.fbx"
+SKIN = SRC / "Skins/humanFemaleA.png"
+OUT = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/female_character.glb"
+
+
+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def make_skin_material():
+    mat = bpy.data.materials.new("Kenney Human Female Skin")
+    mat.use_nodes = True
+    nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
+    links = mat.node_tree.links
+    bsdf = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
+    tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexImage")
+    tex.image = bpy.data.images.load(str(SKIN))
+    tex.extension = "CLIP"
+    links.new(tex.outputs["Color"], bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = 0.72
+    return mat
+
+
+def main():
+    clear_scene()
+    bpy.ops.import_scene.fbx(filepath=str(FBX))
+    mat = make_skin_material()
+
+    for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
+        if obj.type == "MESH":
+            obj.name = f"Female_{obj.name}"
+            obj.data.materials.clear()
+            obj.data.materials.append(mat)
+            obj.location.x = 0
+            obj.location.y = 0
+            obj.location.z = 0
+            obj.modifiers.new("Female_WeightedNormals", "WEIGHTED_NORMAL")
+
+    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
+        filepath=str(OUT),
+        export_format="GLB",
+        export_materials="EXPORT",
+        export_yup=True,
+    )
+    print(f"exported: {OUT}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
*** End Patch

      addCollider(-16, -26, 2.2);

      const carRoot = new THREE.Group();
      carRoot.position.set(pathSamples[0].x, 0, pathSamples[0].y);
      scene.add(carRoot);

      const carGltf = await loader.loadAsync(CAR_URL);
      configure(carGltf.scene);
      const carModel = carGltf.scene;
      carModel.scale.setScalar(3.1);
      carModel.rotation.y = Math.PI;
      const carBox = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(carModel);
      const carCenter = carBox.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
      carModel.position.x -= carCenter.x;
      carModel.position.z -= carCenter.z;
      carModel.position.y -= carBox.min.y;
      carRoot.add(carModel);

      const keys = new Set();
      const car = {
        speed: 0,
        heading: 0.2,
        maxForward: 22,
        maxReverse: -8,
        acceleration: 24,
        brake: 32,
        drag: 7.5,
        steer: 2.25,
        radius: 1.65,
      };

      addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        if (event.key.startsWith("Arrow")) event.preventDefault();
        keys.add(event.key);
      });
      addEventListener("keyup", (event) => keys.delete(event.key));

      function collides(pos) {
        if (Math.abs(pos.x) > MAP_HALF - 4 || Math.abs(pos.z) > MAP_HALF - 4) return true;
        if (Math.abs(pos.x - 42) < 8.6 && Math.abs(pos.z) > 36) return true;
        return colliders.some((item) => (item.x - pos.x) ** 2 + (item.z - pos.z) ** 2 < (item.radius + car.radius) ** 2);
      }

      const clock = new THREE.Clock();
      const cameraTarget = new THREE.Vector3();
      const lookTarget = new THREE.Vector3();

      function update(dt) {
        if (keys.has("ArrowUp")) car.speed += car.acceleration * dt;
        else if (keys.has("ArrowDown")) car.speed -= car.brake * dt;
        else {
          const sign = Math.sign(car.speed);
          car.speed -= sign * car.drag * dt;
          if (Math.sign(car.speed) !== sign) car.speed = 0;
        }

        car.speed = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(car.speed, car.maxReverse, car.maxForward);

        const steerInput = (keys.has("ArrowLeft") ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has("ArrowRight") ? 1 : 0);
        const steerPower = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(car.speed) / 8, 0.2, 1);
        car.heading += steerInput * car.steer * steerPower * dt * Math.sign(car.speed || 1);

        const forward = new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(car.heading), 0, Math.cos(car.heading));
        const next = carRoot.position.clone().addScaledVector(forward, car.speed * dt);
        if (collides(next)) {
          car.speed *= -0.25;
        } else {
          carRoot.position.copy(next);
        }
        carRoot.rotation.y = car.heading;

        const back = new THREE.Vector3(-Math.sin(car.heading), 0, -Math.cos(car.heading));
        cameraTarget.copy(carRoot.position).addScaledVector(back, 12).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 7.5, 0));
        camera.position.lerp(cameraTarget, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
        lookTarget.copy(carRoot.position).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.4, 0));
        camera.lookAt(lookTarget);
        speedLabel.textContent = `${Math.round(Math.abs(car.speed) * 7)} km/h`;
      }

      function animate() {
        const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.033);
        update(dt);
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }

      addEventListener("resize", () => {
        camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
      });

      animate();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+SRC = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/animated-characters-3"
+FBX = SRC / "Model/characterMedium.fbx"
+IDLE = SRC / "Animations/idle.fbx"
+RUN = SRC / "Animations/run.fbx"
+
+
+def clear_scene():
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="SELECT")
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+
+def describe(path):
+    clear_scene()
+    bpy.ops.import_scene.fbx(filepath=str(path))
+    print(f"\nFILE: {path.name}")
+    for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
+        action_name = obj.animation_data.action.name if obj.animation_data and obj.animation_data.action else None
+        print(f"  {obj.name} type={obj.type} action={action_name}")
+        if obj.type == "ARMATURE":
+            print(f"    bones={len(obj.data.bones)}")
+            print("    first bones:", ", ".join([bone.name for bone in obj.data.bones[:12]]))
+
+
+for path in (FBX, IDLE, RUN):
+    describe(path)

      addCollider(-16, -26, 2.2);

      const carRoot = new THREE.Group();
      carRoot.position.set(pathSamples[0].x, 0, pathSamples[0].y);
      scene.add(carRoot);

      const carGltf = await loader.loadAsync(CAR_URL);
      configure(carGltf.scene);
      const carModel = carGltf.scene;
      carModel.scale.setScalar(3.1);
      carModel.rotation.y = Math.PI;
      const carBox = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(carModel);
      const carCenter = carBox.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
      carModel.position.x -= carCenter.x;
      carModel.position.z -= carCenter.z;
      carModel.position.y -= carBox.min.y;
      carRoot.add(carModel);

      const keys = new Set();
      const car = {
        speed: 0,
        heading: 0.2,
        maxForward: 22,
        maxReverse: -8,
        acceleration: 24,
        brake: 32,
        drag: 7.5,
        steer: 2.25,
        radius: 1.65,
      };

      addEventListener("keydown", (event) => {
        if (event.key.startsWith("Arrow")) event.preventDefault();
        keys.add(event.key);
      });
      addEventListener("keyup", (event) => keys.delete(event.key));

      function collides(pos) {
        if (Math.abs(pos.x) > MAP_HALF - 4 || Math.abs(pos.z) > MAP_HALF - 4) return true;
        if (Math.abs(pos.x - 42) < 8.6 && Math.abs(pos.z) > 36) return true;
        return colliders.some((item) => (item.x - pos.x) ** 2 + (item.z - pos.z) ** 2 < (item.radius + car.radius) ** 2);
      }

      const clock = new THREE.Clock();
      const cameraTarget = new THREE.Vector3();
      const lookTarget = new THREE.Vector3();

      function update(dt) {
        if (keys.has("ArrowUp")) car.speed += car.acceleration * dt;
        else if (keys.has("ArrowDown")) car.speed -= car.brake * dt;
        else {
          const sign = Math.sign(car.speed);
          car.speed -= sign * car.drag * dt;
          if (Math.sign(car.speed) !== sign) car.speed = 0;
        }

        car.speed = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(car.speed, car.maxReverse, car.maxForward);

        const steerInput = (keys.has("ArrowLeft") ? 1 : 0) - (keys.has("ArrowRight") ? 1 : 0);
        const steerPower = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(car.speed) / 8, 0.2, 1);
        car.heading += steerInput * car.steer * steerPower * dt * Math.sign(car.speed || 1);

        const forward = new THREE.Vector3(Math.sin(car.heading), 0, Math.cos(car.heading));
        const next = carRoot.position.clone().addScaledVector(forward, car.speed * dt);
        if (collides(next)) {
          car.speed *= -0.25;
        } else {
          carRoot.position.copy(next);
        }
        carRoot.rotation.y = car.heading;

        const back = new THREE.Vector3(-Math.sin(car.heading), 0, -Math.cos(car.heading));
        cameraTarget.copy(carRoot.position).addScaledVector(back, 12).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 7.5, 0));
        camera.position.lerp(cameraTarget, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
        lookTarget.copy(carRoot.position).add(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.4, 0));
        camera.lookAt(lookTarget);
        speedLabel.textContent = `${Math.round(Math.abs(car.speed) * 7)} km/h`;
      }

      function animate() {
        const dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.033);
        update(dt);
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      }

      addEventListener("resize", () => {
        camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
      });

      animate();
    </script>
  </body>
</html>

[621] tool apply_patch call: *** Begin Patch
*** Add File: aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/convert_scifi_girl.py
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BLEND = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/scifi-girl/Scifi_girl.blend"
+OUT = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/scifi-girl/scifi_girl.glb"
+
+
+def main():
+    bpy.ops.wm.open_mainfile(filepath=str(BLEND))
+
+    keep = {"scifi_girl", "rig_base"}
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="DESELECT")
+    for obj in list(bpy.context.scene.objects):
+        if obj.name not in keep:
+            obj.select_set(True)
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+    armature = bpy.data.objects["rig_base"]
+    mesh = bpy.data.objects["scifi_girl"]
+    armature.name = "SciFiGirl_Armature"
+    mesh.name = "SciFiGirl_Mesh"
+
+    if not armature.animation_data:
+        armature.animation_data_create()
+
+    # Export a compact clip set for runtime control.
+    wanted = {
+        "01 idle": "Idle",
+        "03 run": "Run",
+        "04 run turn": "RunTurnRight",
+        "05 run turn (inv)": "RunTurnLeft",
+    }
+
+    armature.animation_data.nla_tracks.clear()
+    for source_name, export_name in wanted.items():
+        action = bpy.data.actions.get(source_name)
+        if not action:
+            continue
+        action.use_fake_user = True
+        action.name = export_name
+        track = armature.animation_data.nla_tracks.new()
+        track.name = export_name
+        strip = track.strips.new(export_name, int(action.frame_range[0]), action)
+        strip.name = export_name
+
+    armature.animation_data.action = bpy.data.actions.get("Idle")
+
+    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
+        filepath=str(OUT),
+        export_format="GLB",
+        export_materials="EXPORT",
+        export_yup=True,
+        export_animations=True,
+        export_anim_single_armature=True,
+        export_nla_strips=True,
+    )
+    print(f"exported: {OUT}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
*** End Patch

*** Add File: aigame/ai-interface/blender-test/convert_scifi_girl.py
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import bpy
+
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BLEND = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/scifi-girl/Scifi_girl.blend"
+OUT = ROOT / "downloaded-assets/characters/scifi-girl/scifi_girl.glb"
+
+
+def main():
+    bpy.ops.wm.open_mainfile(filepath=str(BLEND))
+
+    keep = {"scifi_girl", "rig_base"}
+    bpy.ops.object.select_all(action="DESELECT")
+    for obj in list(bpy.context.scene.objects):
+        if obj.name not in keep:
+            obj.select_set(True)
+    bpy.ops.object.delete()
+
+    armature = bpy.data.objects["rig_base"]
+    mesh = bpy.data.objects["scifi_girl"]
+    armature.name = "SciFiGirl_Armature"
+    mesh.name = "SciFiGirl_Mesh"
+
+    if not armature.animation_data:
+        armature.animation_data_create()
+
+    # Export a compact clip set for runtime control.
+    wanted = {
+        "01 idle": "Idle",
+        "03 run": "Run",
+        "04 run turn": "RunTurnRight",
+        "05 run turn (inv)": "RunTurnLeft",
+    }
+
+    armature.animation_data.nla_tracks.clear()
+    for source_name, export_name in wanted.items():
+        action = bpy.data.actions.get(source_name)
+        if not action:
+            continue
+        action.use_fake_user = True
+        action.name = export_name
+        track = armature.animation_data.nla_tracks.new()
+        track.name = export_name
+        strip = track.strips.new(export_name, int(action.frame_range[0]), action)
+        strip.name = export_name
+
+    armature.animation_data.action = bpy.data.actions.get("Idle")
+
+    OUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    bpy.ops.export_scene.gltf(
+        filepath=str(OUT),
+        export_format="GLB",
+        export_materials="EXPORT",
+        export_yup=True,
+        export_animations=True,
+        export_anim_single_armature=True,
+        export_nla_strips=True,
+    )
+    print(f"exported: {OUT}")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
*** End Patch
